📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 圆二色光谱显示,大多数 突变 降低了融合后核心的稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关,并且报道了野生型 MeV 融合后核心的第一个晶体结构。 [10.1021/acs.biochem.6c00182]。
  • 根据受感染细胞表面积的测量,这两种化合物都减少了病毒传播,降低了病毒滴度,并且在 HRSV 的情况下,以剂量依赖性方式保留了细胞活力。基因集富集分析揭示了 MON-DNJ 调节基因集的特异性富集,表明 宿主 转录反应存在重叠。
  • 该研究包括 16,703 名儿童,其中 81.5% 在活动期间获得了 MR 疫苗。疫苗接种 覆盖率在不同地区之间差异显着,Tabora 和 Pwani 的覆盖率较低,分别为 58.8%(95% CI:51.3%-65.9%)和 61.0%(95% CI:45.0%-75.0%)。Njombe 和 Mbeya 表现出较高的 MR 疫苗接种 覆盖率,分别为 97.4%(95% CI:90.5-99.3%)和 95.6%(95% CI:90.9-97.9%)。家庭财富五分位数和居住地、看护者的教育程度、看护者的年龄及其婚姻状况与坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月龄儿童在活动期间接受 MR 疫苗接种 相关。空间分布揭示了 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的显着聚类(Moran's I = 0.34,p < 0.01)。
  • 所有模型在训练和测试数据上的 AUC-ROC、F1-Score 和 Brier Score 等指标上都表现出相似的性能。圣保罗市大都市区(州首府)和桑托斯(南美最大港口)一致确定 麻疹 风险最高。使用随机森林模型的决策阈值,共有 2 个城市被突出显示为高风险。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Circular dichroism spectroscopy revealed that most mutations decrease postfusion core stability despite their association with enhanced fusion activity, and the first crystal structure of the wild-type MeV postfusion core was reported.
  • Both compounds reduced viral spread as measured by infected cell surface area, decreased viral titers, and, in the case of HRSV, preserved cell viability in a dose-dependent manner. [10.1016/j.antiviral.2026.106492].
  • The study included 16,703 children, of whom 81.5% received the MR vaccine during the campaign.
  • All models showed similar performances across metrics like AUC-ROC, F1-Score and Brier Score on training and test data.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 2026年7月,美国麻疹病例数突破2300例,超过去年全年总数,达到35年来最高水平。特拉华州在确认4例未接种疫苗的成年男性病例后宣布暴发。同时,一项临床前研究显示,口服候选药物GHP-88310可在雪貂模型中阻断麻疹样病毒的传播,为疫情控制带来新希望。
  • 美国2026年麻疹病例达2318例,创35年新高,超过2025年全年总数,45个州受影响[news-93c691887d1536ab][news-a113acda91afe2da]。
  • 特拉华州宣布麻疹暴发,肯特县4名未接种疫苗的成年男性确诊,卫生部门正进行接触者追踪[news-d5cebfdf87376c92][news-7883e79628ef41f0]。
  • 口服抗病毒候选药物GHP-88310在雪貂模型中成功阻断麻疹样病毒的直接接触和空气传播,并缩短传染期[news-9fc4df9541fa7927][news-9a5b6db55d3c5379]。
  • 俄勒冈州因部分家庭不配合隔离,导致40名未接种疫苗的学生和教职员工暴露后被隔离三周,疫情控制面临挑战[news-85011ffe8c14965e]。
  • measles cases surpassed 2,300, exceeding last year's total and reaching a 35-year high. Delaware declared an outbreak after four unvaccinated adult men were confirmed infected.
  • U.S. measles cases reached 2,318 in 2026, a 35-year high, surpassing the 2025 total, with 45 states affected [news-93c691887d1536ab][news-a113acda91afe2da].
  • Delaware declared a measles outbreak after four unvaccinated adult men in Kent County were confirmed; contact tracing is underway [news-d5cebfdf87376c92][news-7883e79628ef41f0].
  • Oral antiviral candidate GHP-88310 blocked both direct contact and airborne transmission of a measles-like virus in ferrets and shortened the contagious period [news-9fc4df9541fa7927][news-9a5b6db55d3c5379].
  • In Oregon, limited cooperation from affected households led to 40 unvaccinated students and staff being quarantined for three weeks after exposure, complicating containment [news-85011ffe8c14965e].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: International journal of infectious diseases : IJID : official publication of the International Society for Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-21  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ijid.2026.108999  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 108999

2026 年卢旺达南部省 Nyanza 区 麻疹 暴发 期间特定年龄的住院动态:对临床管理的影响的生存分析。

Age Specific Hospitalization Dynamics During a Measles Outbreak in Nyanza District, Southern Province, Rwanda, 2026: A Survival Analysis with Implications for Clinical Management.
作者： Frederic Ntirenganya, Ines Itanga, Sandrine Uwamahoro, Bruce Rwagitinywa, Jean Luc Benimana, Hugues Valois Mucunguzi, Edouard Ruseesa, Emerance Hirwa Igihozo, Edson Rwagasore
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹 仍然是 疫苗 可预防的 死亡率 的主要原因,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。尽管卢旺达的常规免疫覆盖率很高,并引入了两剂 麻疹 风疹疫苗接种计划,但持续的免疫缺口继续推动局部 麻疹 的发展。了解年龄如何影响 暴发 期间的住院时间对于优化临床管理和资源分配至关重要,但使用可靠的事件发生时间方法的证据仍然有限。方法:我们对卢旺达南部省 Nyanza 区(2026 年 2 月 16 日至 4 月 17 日)暴发 期间发现的 56 例 麻疹 确诊病例进行了回顾性 队列研究 分析。人口统计、临床和地理数据是从常规 监测 记录中提取的。Kaplan-Meier 生存曲线描述了按年龄组分层的出院时间。结果:56例病例中,9个月以下婴儿所占比例最大(44.6%),其次是15岁以上成人(25%)。地理集群集中在Busasamana 区(51.7%)。年龄显着预测住院时间(对数秩 p = 0.00049),其中 5-15 岁儿童在第 5 天出院速度最快,而 9 个月以下的婴儿出院速度最快。结论:年龄是 麻疹 住院时间的重要决定因素,婴儿和青少年经历的临床病程最长,而学龄儿童恢复最快。这些发现支持 麻疹 暴发 响应期间针对年龄的临床分诊和资源规划。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管卢旺达的常规免疫覆盖率很高,但麻疹仍然是低收入和中等收入国家中疫苗可预防的死亡率的主要原因,而卢旺达持续存在的免疫差距继续推动局部死亡率。该研究旨在解决年龄如何影响 麻疹 暴发 住院时间的知识差距,为临床管理和资源分配提供信息。
设计与对象
对 2026 年 2 月 16 日至 4 月 17 日卢旺达南部省 Nyanza 区 暴发 期间发现的 56 例 队列研究 确诊病例进行了回顾性 队列研究 分析。人口统计学、临床和地理数据从常规 监测 记录中提取。
核心方法
Kaplan-Meier 生存曲线用于描述按年龄组分层的出院时间。该分析重点关注年龄作为住院时间的预测因素,没有指定生存分析之外的实验室分析或统计模型。
主要结果
9 个月以下的婴儿占病例的 44.6%,其次是 15 岁以上的成人(25%)。地理集群集中在布萨萨马纳地区(51.7%)。年龄显着预测住院时间(对数秩 p = 0.00049),5-15 岁儿童恢复最快,9 个月以下婴儿经历的病程最长。
解释与创新
作者将年龄解释为 麻疹 住院时间的重要决定因素,并指出婴儿和青少年的临床病程最长,而学龄儿童恢复最快。这项研究提供了新的证据,使用事件时间方法来量化 麻疹 暴发 期间特定年龄的住院动态。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在 麻疹 麻疹 响应期间进行针对年龄的临床分诊和资源规划,特别是优先考虑婴儿和青少年以加强监测和护理。
局限与证据强度
小样本量 (n=56) 和对常规 监测 数据的依赖限制了普遍性,并可能引入选择或信息偏差。由于回顾性设计且缺乏详细的临床或实验室数据,证据强度被评估为中等。

Age Specific Hospitalization Dynamics During a Measles Outbreak in Nyanza District, Southern Province, Rwanda, 2026: A Survival Analysis with Implications for Clinical Management.

Authors: Frederic Ntirenganya, Ines Itanga, Sandrine Uwamahoro, Bruce Rwagitinywa, Jean Luc Benimana, Hugues Valois Mucunguzi, Edouard Ruseesa, Emerance Hirwa Igihozo, Edson Rwagasore
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Measles remains a leading cause of vaccine-preventable mortality, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. Despite Rwanda's high routine immunization coverage and introduction of a two-dose measles-rubella schedule, persistent immunity gaps continue to drive localized outbreaks. Understanding how age influences hospitalization duration during outbreaks is critical for optimizing clinical management and resource allocation, yet evidence using robust time-to-event methods remains limited. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort analysis of 56 confirmed measles cases identified during an outbreak in Nyanza District, Southern Province, Rwanda (February 16 - April 17, 2026). Demographic, clinical, and geographic data were extracted from routine surveillance records. Kaplan-Meier survival curves described time to hospital discharge stratified by age group. RESULTS: Among the 56 cases, infants under 9 months formed the largest proportion (44.6%), followed by adults over 15 years (25%). Geographic clustering was concentrated in Busasamana sector (51.7%). Age significantly predicted hospitalization duration (log-rank p = 0.00049), with children aged 5-15 years demonstrating the most rapid discharge by day 5, while infants under 9 months. CONCLUSION: Age was a significant determinant of measles hospitalization duration, with infants and adolescents experiencing the most prolonged clinical courses while school-aged children recovered fastest. These findings support age-targeted clinical triage and resource planning during measles outbreak response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a leading cause of vaccine-preventable mortality in low- and middle-income countries despite high routine immunization coverage in Rwanda, where persistent immunity gaps continue to drive localized outbreaks. The study aimed to address the knowledge gap on how age influences hospitalization duration during measles outbreaks to inform clinical management and resource allocation.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort analysis was conducted among 56 confirmed measles cases identified during an outbreak in Nyanza District, Southern Province, Rwanda, from February 16 to April 17, 2026. Demographic, clinical, and geographic data were extracted from routine surveillance records.
Core methods
Kaplan-Meier survival curves were used to describe time to hospital discharge stratified by age group. The analysis focused on age as a predictor of hospitalization duration without specifying laboratory assays or statistical models beyond the survival analysis.
Main results
Infants under 9 months constituted 44.6% of cases, followed by adults over 15 years (25%). Geographic clustering was concentrated in the Busasamana sector (51.7%). Age significantly predicted hospitalization duration (log-rank p = 0.00049), with children aged 5–15 years recovering fastest and infants under 9 months experiencing the most prolonged courses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted age as a significant determinant of measles hospitalization duration, noting that infants and adolescents had the longest clinical courses while school-aged children recovered most rapidly. This study provides new evidence using time-to-event methods to quantify age-specific hospitalization dynamics during a measles outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support age-targeted clinical triage and resource planning during measles outbreak responses, particularly prioritizing infants and adolescents for enhanced monitoring and care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (n=56) and reliance on routine surveillance data limit generalizability and may introduce selection or information biases. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the retrospective design and lack of detailed clinical or laboratory data.

宿主 定向亚胺糖 MON-DNJ 和 NB-DNJ 在体外抑制 麻疹病毒 和人呼吸道合胞病毒复制。

Host-directed iminosugars MON-DNJ and NB-DNJ inhibit measles virus and human respiratory syncytial virus replication in vitro.
作者： Laura L A van Dijk, Katharina S Schmitz, Matteo N Barbaglia, Andrea Magri, Constantina Fotinou, Raymond A. Dwek, Rory D de Vries
摘要中文翻译麻疹病毒 (MeV) 和人呼吸道合胞病毒 (HRSV) 仍然是全球发病率和 死亡率 的重要原因;麻疹 用于未接种疫苗的儿童,HRSV 用于 6 个月以下婴儿、免疫功能低下者和老年人。尽管有 疫苗 和预防性单克隆抗体,但仍然需要新型 抗病毒药物 疗法。MeV 和 HRSV 都依赖于表面糖蛋白的正确糖基化(MeV 为 F 和 H,HRSV 为 F 和 G)来实现受体相互作用、进入、融合和细胞间传播。宿主 内质网 (ER) α-葡萄糖苷酶能够通过钙联蛋白途径正确折叠这些 N 连接病毒包膜糖蛋白。因此,针对这些 宿主 酶提供了一种有前途的广谱 抗病毒药物 策略。在这里,我们证明亚氨基糖 MON-DNJ 和 NB-DNJ(ER α-葡萄糖苷酶 I 和 II 的抑制剂)使用不同的 治疗 方案在体外对 MeV 和 HRSV 发挥 抗病毒药物 活性。根据受感染细胞表面积的测量,这两种化合物都减少了病毒传播,降低了病毒滴度,并且在 HRSV 的情况下,以剂量依赖性方式保留了细胞活力。MeV 和 HRSV 感染数据集的基因集富集分析揭示了 MON-DNJ 调节基因集的特异性富集,表明 宿主 转录反应存在重叠。抗病毒药物 对 HRSV 的活性在人气道类器官中得到证实。这些发现支持进一步开发亚胺糖作为 宿主 定向 抗病毒药物 对抗 MeV 和 HRSV。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹病毒 (MeV) 和人呼吸道合胞病毒 (HRSV) 仍然是全球发病率和 死亡率 的重要原因;麻疹 用于未接种疫苗的儿童,HRSV 用于 6 个月以下婴儿、免疫功能低下者和老年人。尽管有 疫苗 和预防性单克隆抗体,但仍然需要新型 抗病毒药物 疗法。
设计与对象
该研究使用不同的 治疗 方案在体外评估了亚氨基糖 MON-DNJ 和 NB-DNJ 针对 MeV 和 HRSV 的 抗病毒药物 活性,并在人气道类器官中证实了对 HRSV 的活性。
核心方法
MeV 和 HRSV 都依赖于表面糖蛋白的正确糖基化来实现受体相互作用、进入、融合和细胞间扩散,并且 宿主 内质网 (ER) α-葡萄糖苷酶能够通过钙联蛋白途径实现正确折叠。
主要结果
根据受感染细胞表面积的测量,这两种化合物都减少了病毒传播,降低了病毒滴度,并且在 HRSV 的情况下,以剂量依赖性方式保留了细胞活力。基因集富集分析揭示了 MON-DNJ 调节基因集的特异性富集,表明 宿主 转录反应存在重叠。
解释与创新
靶向 宿主 酶提供了一种有前途的广谱 抗病毒药物 策略,这些发现支持亚胺糖的进一步开发,如 宿主 定向的 抗病毒药物 对抗 MeV 和 HRSV。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为针对 MeV 和 HRSV 的 宿主 定向 抗病毒药物 的潜在开发提供了信息,这可以解决这些重要的全球病原体当前治疗方案的差距。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在体外和人体气道类器官中进行的,限制了对体内环境的推广;然而,由于直接观察到 抗病毒药物 活性和剂量依赖性效应,证据强度很高。

Host-directed iminosugars MON-DNJ and NB-DNJ inhibit measles virus and human respiratory syncytial virus replication in vitro.

Authors: Laura L A van Dijk, Katharina S Schmitz, Matteo N Barbaglia, Andrea Magri, Constantina Fotinou, Raymond A. Dwek, Rory D de Vries
Original abstract (English)
Measles virus (MeV) and human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) remain significant causes of global morbidity and mortality; measles in unvaccinated children, HRSV in infants below 6 months, immunocompromised, and the elderly. Despite the availability of vaccines and prophylactic monoclonal antibodies, there is a need for novel antiviral therapeutics. Both MeV and HRSV depend on correct glycosylation of surface glycoproteins (F and H for MeV, F and G for HRSV) for receptor interaction, entry, fusion, and cell-to-cell spread. Host endoplasmic reticulum (ER) α-glucosidases enable the proper folding of these N-linked viral enveloped glycoproteins via the calnexin pathway. Therefore, targeting those host-enzymes offers a promising broad-spectrum antiviral strategy. Here we demonstrate that the iminosugars MON-DNJ and NB-DNJ, which are inhibitors of ER α-glucosidases I and II, exert antiviral activity against both MeV and HRSV in vitro using different treatment protocols. Both compounds reduced viral spread as measured by infected cell surface area, decreased viral titers, and, in the case of HRSV, preserved cell viability in a dose-dependent manner. Gene set enrichment analysis of MeV and HRSV infection datasets revealed specific enrichment of MON-DNJ-modulated gene set, suggesting overlap in host transcriptional response. Antiviral activity to HRSV was confirmed in human airway organoids. These findings support the further development of iminosugars as host-directed antivirals against MeV and HRSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles virus (MeV) and human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) remain significant causes of global morbidity and mortality; measles in unvaccinated children, HRSV in infants below 6 months, immunocompromised, and the elderly. Despite the availability of vaccines and prophylactic monoclonal antibodies, there is a need for novel antiviral therapeutics.
Design and population
The study evaluated the antiviral activity of iminosugars MON-DNJ and NB-DNJ against MeV and HRSV in vitro using different treatment protocols, with activity confirmed in human airway organoids for HRSV.
Core methods
Both MeV and HRSV depend on correct glycosylation of surface glycoproteins for receptor interaction, entry, fusion, and cell-to-cell spread, and host endoplasmic reticulum (ER) α-glucosidases enable proper folding via the calnexin pathway.
Main results
Both compounds reduced viral spread as measured by infected cell surface area, decreased viral titers, and, in the case of HRSV, preserved cell viability in a dose-dependent manner. Gene set enrichment analysis revealed specific enrichment of MON-DNJ-modulated gene set, suggesting overlap in host transcriptional response.
Interpretation and novelty
Targeting host-enzymes offers a promising broad-spectrum antiviral strategy, and these findings support the further development of iminosugars as host-directed antivirals against MeV and HRSV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the potential development of host-directed antivirals for MeV and HRSV, which could address gaps in current therapeutic options for these significant global pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro and in human airway organoids, limiting generalizability to in vivo settings; however, the evidence strength is high due to direct observation of antiviral activity and dose-dependent effects.

麻疹 类病毒的持久性、多物种 暴发 和远距离 传播

Persistent, Multi-Species Outbreaks and Long-Range Transmissions of a Measles-Like Virus
作者： Nicole Nova, Katherine A. Solari, Kimberlee B. Beckmen, Martin Gilbert, Ellen E. Brandell, Julia A. Palacios, Elizabeth A. Hadly, Erin A. Mordecai, Dmitri A. Petrov
摘要中文翻译犬瘟热是一种类似 麻疹 的疾病,死亡率 较高,目前尚无治愈方法,对全球野生和家养食肉动物构成重大威胁。家犬通常被认为是该疾病的主要 储存宿主 和远程传播者,但野生动物的作用可能被低估,并且 CDV 在野生动物中的长期持续性从未得到评估。我们对十多年来(2012--2021 年)从阿拉斯加和黄石公园的北极狐和其他犬科动物身上采集的犬瘟热病毒 (CDV) 完整和部分 基因组 样本进行了测序,并编制了全球 32 个物种中所有已发布的 CDV 基因组 样本的数据集。我们首次展示了 CDV 在野生动物中持续存在的证据(近十年),其爆炸性的 传播 动态跨越了 宿主 物种障碍。从北极采样的菌株首次将北美与欧亚大陆连接起来,并且与黄石菌株和其他已知的北美谱系不同。这表明不同的野生动物 暴发 同时出现在北美,可能是从欧亚大陆引入的。我们记录到的这种毁灭性的泛动物病毒在野生动物体内的长期持续存在、远距离移动和爆炸性传播令人震惊,并强调需要加强监测工作,以更好地保护全球野生动物种群。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
犬瘟热病毒 (CDV) 是一种具有高 死亡率 的 麻疹 样病原体,威胁着全球野生和家养食肉动物,但其在野生动物中的长期存在以及野生动物 死亡率 的被低估的作用仍然知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究对十多年来(2012-2021)从阿拉斯加和黄石公园的北极狐和其他犬科动物身上采集的 CDV 基因组 样本进行了测序,并编制了涵盖 32 个物种的所有已发布 CDV 基因组 样本的全球数据集。
核心方法
从现场样本中对 CDV 基因组 进行了完整和部分测序,并组装了比较基因组数据集,以评估 宿主 物种和地理区域的系统发育关系和 传播 动态。
主要结果
该研究首次记录了CDV在野生动物中长期存在的证据(近十年)、跨越宿主物种屏障的爆炸性传播动态,以及来自连接北美和欧亚大陆的北极地区与黄石和其他北美谱系不同的毒株。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了北美同时存在的野生动物暴发,可能是从欧亚大陆引入的,强调了野生动物种群中以前未被识别的长距离运动和持续的传播。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,迫切需要加强野生动物中的 监测 和 CDV 监测,以减轻泛动物疫情的传播并保护全球脆弱的食肉动物种群。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于基因组采样和系统发育推断,没有实验 传播 或干预数据,将因果推断和概括性限制在更广泛的 宿主 范围内。

Persistent, Multi-Species Outbreaks and Long-Range Transmissions of a Measles-Like Virus

Authors: Nicole Nova, Katherine A. Solari, Kimberlee B. Beckmen, Martin Gilbert, Ellen E. Brandell, Julia A. Palacios, Elizabeth A. Hadly, Erin A. Mordecai, Dmitri A. Petrov
Original abstract (English)
Canine distemper---a measles-like disease with high mortality and presently without a cure---poses a major threat to wild and domestic carnivores globally. Domestic dogs are generally considered to be the main reservoir and long-range transmitter of the disease, but the role of wildlife is likely underestimated and the long-term persistence of CDV in wildlife has never been assessed. We sequenced canine distemper virus (CDV) full and partial genomes that were sampled over a decade (2012--2021) from Arctic foxes and other canids in Alaska and Yellowstone, and compiled a dataset of all published CDV genomes sampled across 32 species globally. We show the first ever evidence of persistence of CDV in wildlife (for almost a decade) with explosive transmission dynamics crossing host species barriers. Strains sampled from the Arctic for the first time connect North America to Eurasia, and are distinct from the Yellowstone strain and other known North American lineages. This suggests that separate wildlife outbreaks occur concurrently in North America, with possible introductions from Eurasia. The long-term persistence, long-range movement and explosive spread of this devastating panzootic virus that we document within wildlife is alarming and highlights the need for increased monitoring efforts to better protect wildlife populations globally.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Canine distemper virus (CDV), a measles-like pathogen with high mortality, threatens wild and domestic carnivores globally, yet its long-term persistence in wildlife and the underestimated role of wildlife reservoirs remain poorly understood.
Design and population
The study sequenced CDV genomes sampled over a decade (2012–2021) from Arctic foxes and other canids in Alaska and Yellowstone, and compiled a global dataset of all published CDV genomes sampled across 32 species.
Core methods
Full and partial CDV genomes were sequenced from field samples, and a comparative genomic dataset was assembled to assess phylogenetic relationships and transmission dynamics across host species and geographic regions.
Main results
The study documented the first evidence of long-term persistence of CDV in wildlife (nearly a decade), explosive transmission dynamics across host species barriers, and strains from the Arctic connecting North America to Eurasia that are distinct from Yellowstone and other North American lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal concurrent wildlife outbreaks in North America with possible introductions from Eurasia, highlighting previously unrecognized long-range movements and persistent transmission in wildlife populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the urgent need for enhanced surveillance and monitoring of CDV in wildlife to mitigate panzootic spread and protect vulnerable carnivore populations globally.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on genomic sampling and phylogenetic inference without experimental transmission or intervention data, limiting causal inference and generalizability to broader host ranges.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

影响坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月儿童 2024 年 麻疹-风疹 疫苗接种 运动覆盖率的空间分布和相关因素:多层次混合效应分析。

Spatial distribution and associated factors influencing 2024 Measles-Rubella vaccination campaign coverage among children aged 9-59 months in Mainland Tanzania: A multi-level mixed-effect analysis.
作者： Hajirani M. Msuya, Samwel Lwambura, Ibrahim Msuya, Omar Lweno, Bakari Fakih, Mwifadhi Mrisho, Hassan Tearish, Gumi Abdallah, Selemani Mmbaga, August Kuwawenaruwa
摘要中文翻译简介:在全球范围内,麻疹 仍然是儿童 死亡率 的主要原因,而风疹是所有传染病中导致出生缺陷的主要原因。在坦桑尼亚大陆,由于地理多样性、医疗设施分布不均以及各地区社会经济差异,消除 麻疹 和风疹仍然具有挑战性。了解 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的空间模式和相关决定因素对于提高活动有效性至关重要。本研究旨在探讨坦桑尼亚大陆9-59个月儿童麻疹风疹运动覆盖率的空间分布及相关因素。方法:在 2024 年 2 月实施 麻疹 风疹 (MR) 疫苗接种 运动后进行了一项横断面调查,以评估坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月龄儿童 MR 疫苗接种 覆盖率的空间分布和影响因素。使用 Moran's I 评估空间自相关性以检测聚类,同时使用空间关联局部指标 (LISA) 来识别高-高和低-低聚类。使用 Getis-Ord Gi* 统计数据以 95% 的置信水平进行热点和冷点分析,与标准流行病学报告实践一致,以确定具有统计显着性的空间集群。为了确定与活动覆盖范围相关的因素,我们使用了多变量逻辑回归模型。结果:该研究纳入了 16,703 名儿童,其中 81.5% 在活动期间接受了 MR 疫苗。疫苗接种 覆盖率在不同地区之间差异显着,Tabora 和 Pwani 的覆盖率较低,分别为 58.8%(95% CI:51.3%-65.9%)和 61.0%(95% CI:45.0%-75.0%)。Njombe 和 Mbeya 表现出较高的 MR 疫苗接种 覆盖率,分别为 97.4%(95% CI:90.5-99.3%)和 95.6%(95% CI:90.9-97.9%)。家庭财富五分位数和居住地、看护者的教育程度、看护者的年龄及其婚姻状况与坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月龄儿童在活动期间接受 MR 疫苗接种 相关。空间分布揭示了 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的显着聚类(Moran's I = 0.34,p < 0.01)。LISA 确定了两种不同的集群类别:高-高集群(高覆盖率)和低-低集群(低覆盖率)。高-高聚类表示 MR 疫苗 率高的区域,周围有相似的邻居,集中在 Njombe 和姆贝亚部分地区,而低-低聚类表示 MR 覆盖率低的区域,出现在 Tabora、Katavi、Dar es Salaam 和 Pwani 等地区。Getis-Ord Gi* 热点分析显示出显着的聚类,其中高置信度热点位于南部高地(Njombe 和 Mbeya),而显着的冷点位于坦桑尼亚西部(Tabora 和 Katavi)以及坦桑尼亚东部部分地区。结论:本研究表明,坦桑尼亚大陆的 麻疹-风疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围存在显着的空间异质性,社会经济和人口因素导致持续存在地理不平等。这些发现表明,将地理空间见解与以公平为中心的规划相结合如何能够支持精准的公共卫生规划,从而能够采取有针对性的干预措施来缩小覆盖范围差距并加速 麻疹 和消除风疹的进展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在全球范围内,麻疹 仍然是儿童 死亡率 的主要原因,风疹是所有传染病中导致出生缺陷的主要原因。在坦桑尼亚大陆,由于地理多样性、医疗设施分布不均以及各地区社会经济差异,消除 麻疹 和风疹仍然具有挑战性。
设计与对象
2024 年 2 月开展 麻疹 风疹 (MR) 疫苗接种 运动后开展了一项横断面调查,以评估坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月龄儿童 MR 疫苗接种 覆盖率的空间分布和影响因素。
核心方法
使用 Moran's I 评估空间自相关性以检测聚类,同时使用空间关联局部指标 (LISA) 来识别高-高和低-低聚类。使用 Getis-Ord Gi* 统计数据以 95% 的置信水平进行热点和冷点分析,与标准流行病学报告实践一致,以确定具有统计显着性的空间集群。为了确定与活动覆盖范围相关的因素,我们使用了多变量逻辑回归模型。
主要结果
该研究包括 16,703 名儿童,其中 81.5% 在活动期间获得了 MR 疫苗。疫苗接种 覆盖率在不同地区之间差异显着,Tabora 和 Pwani 的覆盖率较低,分别为 58.8%(95% CI:51.3%-65.9%)和 61.0%(95% CI:45.0%-75.0%)。Njombe 和 Mbeya 表现出较高的 MR 疫苗接种 覆盖率,分别为 97.4%(95% CI:90.5-99.3%)和 95.6%(95% CI:90.9-97.9%)。家庭财富五分位数和居住地、看护者的教育程度、看护者的年龄及其婚姻状况与坦桑尼亚大陆 9-59 个月龄儿童在活动期间接受 MR 疫苗接种 相关。空间分布揭示了 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的显着聚类(Moran's I = 0.34,p < 0.01)。
解释与创新
本研究表明,坦桑尼亚大陆的 麻疹-风疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围存在显着的空间异质性,社会经济和人口因素导致持续存在地理不平等。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,将地理空间见解与以公平为中心的规划相结合如何能够支持精准的公共卫生规划,从而能够采取有针对性的干预措施来缩小覆盖范围差距并加速 麻疹 和消除风疹的进展。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Spatial distribution and associated factors influencing 2024 Measles-Rubella vaccination campaign coverage among children aged 9-59 months in Mainland Tanzania: A multi-level mixed-effect analysis.

Authors: Hajirani M. Msuya, Samwel Lwambura, Ibrahim Msuya, Omar Lweno, Bakari Fakih, Mwifadhi Mrisho, Hassan Tearish, Gumi Abdallah, Selemani Mmbaga, August Kuwawenaruwa
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Globally, measles remains a major cause of child mortality, and rubella is the leading cause of birth defects among all infectious diseases. In Mainland Tanzania, eliminating measles and rubella remains challenging due to geographical diversity, uneven healthcare facilities distribution, and socio-economic disparities across regions. Understanding spatial patterns and associated determinants of vaccination coverage is essential for improving campaign effectiveness. This study aimed to explore the spatial distribution and associated factors of measles-rubella campaign coverage among children aged 9-59 months in Mainland Tanzania. METHODS: A cross-sectional survey was conducted following the implementation of the February 2024 measles-rubella (MR) vaccination campaign to assess the spatial distribution and factors influencing MR vaccination coverage among children aged 9-59 months in Mainland Tanzania. Spatial autocorrelation was evaluated using Moran's I to detect clustering, while Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA) was used to identify High-High and Low-Low clusters. Hotspot and cold-spot analyses were performed using Getis-Ord Gi* statistics at the 95% confidence level, consistent with standard epidemiological reporting practices, to identify statistically significant spatial clusters. To identify factors associated with the campaign coverage, we used a multivariable logistic regression model. RESULTS: The study included 16,703 children, of whom 81.5% received the MR vaccine during the campaign. Vaccination coverage varied notably between regions, with Tabora and Pwani having a low coverage rate of 58.8% (95% CI: 51.3%-65.9%) and 61.0% (95% CI: 45.0%-75.0%) respectively. Njombe and Mbeya demonstrated high MR vaccination coverage of 97.4% (95% CI: 90.5-99.3%) and 95.6% (95% CI: 90.9-97.9%), respectively. The household wealth quintile and place of residence, caregiver's education, caregiver's age, and their marital status were associated with receiving MR vaccination during the campaign among children aged 9-59 months in Mainland Tanzania. Spatial distribution revealed significant clustering of vaccination coverage (Moran's I = 0.34, p < 0.01). The LISA identified two distinct categories of clusters: High-High Clusters (high-coverage) and Low-Low Clusters (low-coverage). High-High Clusters, which indicate regions with high MR vaccine rates surrounded by similar neighbors, are concentrated in regions such as Njombe and parts of Mbeya, whereas Low-Low Clusters, representing regions with low MR coverage, are found in areas like Tabora, Katavi, Dar es Salaam and Pwani. The Getis-Ord Gi* hotspot analysis shows significant clustering, with high-confidence hotspots in the southern highlands (Njombe and Mbeya) and notable cold spots in western Tanzania (Tabora and Katavi) and parts of eastern Tanzania. CONCLUSION: This study demonstrates substantial spatial heterogeneity in measles-rubella vaccination coverage across Mainland Tanzania, with persistent geographic inequities driven by socio-economic and demographic factors. These findings demonstrate how integrating geospatial insights with equity-focused planning can support precision public health planning, enabling targeted interventions to close coverage gaps and accelerate progress toward measles and rubella elimination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Globally, measles remains a major cause of child mortality, and rubella is the leading cause of birth defects among all infectious diseases. In Mainland Tanzania, eliminating measles and rubella remains challenging due to geographical diversity, uneven healthcare facilities distribution, and socio-economic disparities across regions.

Hotspot and cold-spot analyses were performed using Getis-Ord Gi* statistics at the 95% confidence level, consistent with standard epidemiological reporting practices, to identify statistically significant spatial clusters. To identify factors associated with the campaign coverage, we used a multivariable logistic regression model.

The study included 16,703 children, of whom 81.5% received the MR vaccine during the campaign. Vaccination coverage varied notably between regions, with Tabora and Pwani having a low coverage rate of 58.8% (95% CI: 51.3%-65.9%) and 61.0% (95% CI: 45.0%-75.0%) respectively. The spatial distribution revealed significant clustering of vaccination coverage (Moran's I = 0.34, p < 0.01).

This study demonstrates substantial spatial heterogeneity in measles-rubella vaccination coverage across Mainland Tanzania, with persistent geographic inequities driven by socio-economic and demographic factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings demonstrate how integrating geospatial insights with equity-focused planning can support precision public health planning, enabling targeted interventions to close coverage gaps and accelerate progress toward measles and rubella elimination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

使用机器学习估计圣保罗 麻疹 重新引入风险。

Estimating measles reintroduction risk in São Paulo using machine learning.
作者： Denise Cammarota, Danilo Pereira Mori, Flávia Cristina da Silva Sales, Isabela Galvão, Alícia Tavares da Silva Gomes, Frederico Prado, Ramon Wilk da Silva, Raquel Gardini Sanches Palasio, Pamella Cristina de Carvalho Lucas, Telma Regina Marques Pinto Carvalhanas
摘要中文翻译目标:本研究旨在开发 麻疹 风险评估工具来预测巴西人口最多的州圣保罗的市级事件。研究设计:通过使用 2007 年至 2023 年的公共数据,并通过机器学习模型的使用和选择进行整合,估算了圣保罗州 645 个城市的 麻疹 风险。方法:使用公开的人口统计、流动性、社会经济和 麻疹 数据,通过 K 倍分层交叉验证训练五个机器学习预测模型,以预测每个城市每年的 麻疹 发生率。将城市划分为高/低风险的模型特定决策阈值是从根据验证数据评估的接收者操作特征 (ROC) 曲线获得的。使用测试数据的沙普利加性解释 (SHAP) 来识别最有影响力的特征。结果:所有模型在训练和测试数据上的 AUC-ROC、F1-Score 和 Brier Score 等指标上都表现出相似的性能。圣保罗市大都市区(州首府)和桑托斯(南美最大港口)一致确定 麻疹 风险最高。使用随机森林模型的决策阈值,共有 2 个城市被突出显示为高风险。国际机场的旅客到达量、世卫组织报告的病例以及城市人口规模和人口密度是最有影响力的特征,这与圣保罗州当前非流行性 麻疹 情况一致。结论:建议使用 麻疹 风险估计工具来估计圣保罗州的 麻疹 风险。由于它依赖于公开数据,因此拟议的框架可以作为巴西其他州或地区类似举措的基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发 麻疹 风险评估工具来预测巴西人口最多的州圣保罗的市级事件。
设计与对象
圣保罗州 645 个城市的 麻疹 风险是通过使用 2007 年至 2023 年的公共数据进行估计的,并通过机器学习模型的使用和选择进行整合。
核心方法
使用公开的人口统计、流动性、社会经济和 疫苗接种 数据,通过 K 倍分层交叉验证训练五个机器学习预测模型,以预测每个城市每年的 麻疹 发生率。将城市划分为高/低风险的模型特定决策阈值是从根据验证数据评估的接收者操作特征 (ROC) 曲线获得的。使用测试数据的沙普利加性解释 (SHAP) 来识别最有影响力的特征。
主要结果
所有模型在训练和测试数据上的 AUC-ROC、F1-Score 和 Brier Score 等指标上都表现出相似的性能。圣保罗市大都市区(州首府)和桑托斯(南美最大港口)一致确定 麻疹 风险最高。使用随机森林模型的决策阈值,共有 2 个城市被突出显示为高风险。
解释与创新
提出了一种 麻疹 风险估计工具,用于估计圣保罗州的 麻疹 风险。由于它依赖于公开数据,因此拟议的框架可以作为巴西其他州或地区类似举措的基础。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为高风险城市的针对性 麻疹 和预防策略提供信息,从而有可能改善圣保罗州的准备和响应。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于公开数据,这些数据在完整性或准确性方面可能存在局限性。证据强度中等,因为模型表现出一致的性能,但在非流行环境中的历史数据上进行了验证。

Estimating measles reintroduction risk in São Paulo using machine learning.

Authors: Denise Cammarota, Danilo Pereira Mori, Flávia Cristina da Silva Sales, Isabela Galvão, Alícia Tavares da Silva Gomes, Frederico Prado, Ramon Wilk da Silva, Raquel Gardini Sanches Palasio, Pamella Cristina de Carvalho Lucas, Telma Regina Marques Pinto Carvalhanas
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to develop a measles risk estimation tool to predict municipal-level occurrences in Brazil's most populous state, São Paulo. STUDY DESIGN: Measles risk for the 645 municipalities of the State of São Paulo was estimated by using public data spanning 2007-2023, integrated via the use and selection of machine learning models. METHODS: Five machine-learning prediction models were trained via K-fold stratified cross validation using publicly available demographic, mobility, socioeconomic, and vaccination data to predict annual measles occurrence per municipality. Model-specific decision thresholds to classify municipalities into high/low risk were obtained from the Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve evaluated on validation data. Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) on test data were used to identify the most influential features. RESULTS: All models showed similar performances across metrics like AUC-ROC, F1-Score and Brier Score on training and test data. The highest measles risk was consistently identified in the São Paulo municipality metropolitan region (state capital) and in Santos (largest South American port). Using the decision threshold for the Random Forest model, a total of 2 municipalities were highlighted as high risk. Passenger arrivals at international airports, WHO-reported cases along with municipal population size and population density were the most influential features, consistent with the current non-endemic measles scenario in São Paulo State. CONCLUSIONS: A measles risk-estimation tool is proposed for estimating measles risk in São Paulo State. Because it relies on publicly available data, the proposed framework may serve as a basis for similar initiatives in other Brazilian states or regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a measles risk estimation tool to predict municipal-level occurrences in Brazil's most populous state, São Paulo.
Design and population
Measles risk for the 645 municipalities of the State of São Paulo was estimated by using public data spanning 2007-2023, integrated via the use and selection of machine learning models.
Core methods
Five machine-learning prediction models were trained via K-fold stratified cross validation using publicly available demographic, mobility, socioeconomic, and vaccination data to predict annual measles occurrence per municipality. Model-specific decision thresholds to classify municipalities into high/low risk were obtained from the Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve evaluated on validation data. Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) on test data were used to identify the most influential features.
Main results
All models showed similar performances across metrics like AUC-ROC, F1-Score and Brier Score on training and test data. The highest measles risk was consistently identified in the São Paulo municipality metropolitan region (state capital) and in Santos (largest South American port). Using the decision threshold for the Random Forest model, a total of 2 municipalities were highlighted as high risk.
Interpretation and novelty
A measles risk-estimation tool is proposed for estimating measles risk in São Paulo State. Because it relies on publicly available data, the proposed framework may serve as a basis for similar initiatives in other Brazilian states or regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies in high-risk municipalities, potentially improving measles outbreak preparedness and response in São Paulo State.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on publicly available data, which may have limitations in completeness or accuracy. The evidence strength is moderate, as the models showed consistent performance but were validated on historical data in a non-endemic setting.

超融合 麻疹病毒 融合糖蛋白的融合后六螺旋束不稳定。

Hyperfusogenic Mutations Destabilize the Postfusion Six-Helix Bundle of the Measles Virus Fusion Glycoprotein.
作者： Nisansala Vithanage, Victor K. Outlaw
摘要中文翻译I 类病毒融合蛋白的 宿主 膜和病毒包膜的融合是由融合后六螺旋束的组装驱动的,该六螺旋束是通过 N 端 (HR1) 和 C 端 (HR2) 七肽重复区域之间的反平行相互作用形成的。尽管已知 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 融合 (F) 糖蛋白这些区域中的 突变 会促进神经病理性和过度融合表型,但它们对融合后核心稳定性的影响尚未得到系统检查。在这里,我们结合肽生物物理学和 X 射线晶体学来探究 HR2 结构域内的 突变 如何影响融合后 6HB 组装,突变 存在于天然神经致病性 MeV 分离株(例如 L454W 和 N462K)和实验室产生的超融合变体(例如 L454M 和 T461A)中。圆二色性 (CD) 光谱表明,除了少数例外,这些 突变 会降低融合后核心稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关。我们还报告了野生型 MeV 融合后核心的第一个晶体结构以及六种超融合变体的结构,从而能够对不稳定的分子基础进行高分辨率比较。结构分析表明,这些效应是由 HR2 内的空间堆积、氢键网络和螺旋稳定相互作用的局部扰动引起的,而整体 6HB 结构仍然保守。总之,这些结果表明,超融合性 突变 与融合后状态的稳定无关,而是与融合能量障碍减少而产生的超融合性模型一致,可能是通过对融合前稳定性或触发效率的影响。这些发现建立了控制卷曲螺旋组装的关键序列-结构-稳定性关系,并为基于 HR1 和 HR2 的融合抑制剂的设计提供了框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管已知 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 融合糖蛋白中的 突变 会促进神经病理性和高融合表型,但它们对融合后核心稳定性的影响尚未得到系统研究。
设计与对象
该研究结合肽生物物理学和 X 射线晶体学,研究 MeV 融合糖蛋白 HR2 结构域内的 突变,包括来自天然神经病原分离物和实验室产生的超融合变体的 突变。
核心方法
圆二色性 (CD) 光谱用于评估融合后核心稳定性,X 射线晶体学用于确定野生型 MeV 融合后核心和六种超融合变体的结构。
主要结果
圆二色光谱显示,大多数 突变 降低了融合后核心的稳定性,尽管它们与增强的融合活性有关,并且报道了野生型 MeV 融合后核心的第一个晶体结构。
解释与创新
作者解释说,超融合 突变 与融合后状态的稳定无关,而是可能通过影响融合前稳定性或触发效率来减少融合的能量障碍。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现建立了控制卷曲螺旋组装的关键序列-结构-稳定性关系,并为基于 HR1 和 HR2 的融合抑制剂的设计提供了框架。
局限与证据强度
由于生物物理和结构方法的结合,证据强度很高,尽管该研究侧重于特定的 突变 并且可能无法推广到所有超融合变体。

Hyperfusogenic Mutations Destabilize the Postfusion Six-Helix Bundle of the Measles Virus Fusion Glycoprotein.

Authors: Nisansala Vithanage, Victor K. Outlaw
Original abstract (English)
Fusion of the host membrane and viral envelope by class I viral fusion proteins is driven by the assembly of a postfusion six-helix bundle formed through antiparallel interactions between N-terminal (HR1) and C-terminal (HR2) heptad-repeat regions. Although mutations in these regions of the measles virus (MeV) fusion (F) glycoprotein are known to promote neuropathogenic and hyperfusogenic phenotypes, their effects on postfusion core stability have not been systematically examined. Here, we combine peptide biophysics and X-ray crystallography to interrogate how mutations within the HR2 domain, present in native neuropathogenic MeV isolates ( e.g ., L454W and N462K) and laboratory-generated hyperfusogenic variants ( e.g ., L454M and T461A), influence postfusion 6HB assembly. Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy reveals that, with few exceptions, these mutations decrease postfusion core stability, despite their association with enhanced fusion activity. We also report the first crystal structure of the wild-type MeV postfusion core as well as structures of six hyperfusogenic variants, enabling high-resolution comparison of the molecular basis of destabilization. Structural analysis shows that these effects arise from localized perturbations to steric packing, hydrogen bonding networks, and helix-stabilizing interactions within HR2, while the overall 6HB architecture remains conserved. Together, these results indicate that hyperfusogenic mutations are not associated with stabilization of the postfusion state and are instead consistent with models in which hyperfusogenicity arises from a reduction in the energetic barrier to fusion, potentially through effects on prefusion stability or triggering efficiency. These findings establish key sequence-structure-stability relationships governing coiled-coil assembly and provide a framework for the design of HR1- and HR2-based fusion inhibitors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Although mutations in the measles virus (MeV) fusion glycoprotein are known to promote neuropathogenic and hyperfusogenic phenotypes, their effects on postfusion core stability have not been systematically examined.
Design and population
The study combined peptide biophysics and X-ray crystallography to investigate mutations within the HR2 domain of the MeV fusion glycoprotein, including those from native neuropathogenic isolates and laboratory-generated hyperfusogenic variants.
Core methods
Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy was used to assess postfusion core stability, and X-ray crystallography was employed to determine the structures of the wild-type MeV postfusion core and six hyperfusogenic variants.
Main results
Circular dichroism spectroscopy revealed that most mutations decrease postfusion core stability despite their association with enhanced fusion activity, and the first crystal structure of the wild-type MeV postfusion core was reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that hyperfusogenic mutations are not associated with stabilization of the postfusion state but instead reduce the energetic barrier to fusion, potentially through effects on prefusion stability or triggering efficiency.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings establish key sequence-structure-stability relationships governing coiled-coil assembly and provide a framework for the design of HR1- and HR2-based fusion inhibitors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high due to the combination of biophysical and structural methods, though the study focuses on specific mutations and may not generalize to all hyperfusogenic variants.

工程溶瘤 麻疹病毒 与 MG53 将焦亡肿瘤杀伤与免疫微环境重塑相结合,以增强非小细胞肺癌的检查点免疫治疗

Engineering Oncolytic Measles Virus with MG53 Couples Pyroptotic Tumor Killing with Immune Microenvironment Remodeling to Enhance Checkpoint Immunotherapy in Non-small Cell Lung Cancer
作者： Zhongguang Li, Cheng Chih Hsu, Fei Jiang, Matthew Bu, Umme Lubaba, Sally L. Li, Li Zhao, Pei-Hui Lin, Hunter J. Piegols, Xuefeng Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 肺癌是全球癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因,只有约 30% 的患者从目前的免疫检查点免疫治疗中受益。溶瘤病毒疗法为克服这种耐药性提供了一种有前景的策略;然而,实现有效的肿瘤细胞毒性和强大的抗肿瘤免疫激活仍然是一个重大挑战。在这里,我们设计了表达肿瘤抑制因子 MG53 (TRIM72) 的溶瘤 麻疹病毒,命名为 rMeV-MG53,并评估了其对非小细胞肺癌 (NSCLC) 的治疗潜力。rMeV-MG53 保留了与亲代 MeV 相当的复制动力学,同时在 NSCLC 细胞中比未武装的对照具有显着更大的细胞毒性。从机制上讲,MG53 武装放大了 caspase-3 依赖性细胞凋亡并增强了 Gasdermin E (GSDME) 裂解,使 GSDME 介导的焦亡成为关键的肿瘤杀伤机制;药理学抑制证实 caspase 依赖性细胞凋亡是导致细胞焦亡的起始事件,同时排除坏死性凋亡和铁死亡作为 rMeV-MG53 诱导的细胞毒性的重要贡献者。MG53 过表达诱导了富含 TNF、NF-κB 和 IL-17 信号传导的内在促炎转录特征,而 rMeV-MG53 比未武装对照诱导了显着更强的 I 型干扰素和促炎细胞因子诱导。在免疫活性、MeV 允许的同基因 NSCLC 模型中,瘤内 rMeV-MG53 实现了卓越的肿瘤生长抑制,放大了 caspase-3/GSDME 焦亡,选择性上调 Cxcl10 、 Ifng 和 Il1b,并驱动强大的 CD8+ T 细胞和颗粒酶 B+ 效应器浸润。这种免疫重塑伴随着补偿性 PD-L1 上调,rMeV-MG53 与抗 PD-L1 阻断相结合,实现了所有 治疗 组中最强的肿瘤抑制。这些发现确立了携带 MG53 的溶瘤 MeV 作为一种将 GSDME 介导的细胞焦亡与抗肿瘤免疫重塑相结合的策略,以使免疫抵抗性 NSCLC 对检查点免疫治疗敏感。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肺癌是全球癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因,只有约 30% 的患者从目前的免疫检查点免疫治疗中受益。溶瘤病毒疗法为克服这种耐药性提供了一种有前景的策略;然而,实现有效的肿瘤细胞毒性和强大的抗肿瘤免疫激活仍然是一个重大挑战。
设计与对象
在这里,我们设计了表达肿瘤抑制因子 MG53 (TRIM72) 的溶瘤 麻疹病毒,命名为 rMeV-MG53,并评估了其对非小细胞肺癌 (NSCLC) 的治疗潜力。
核心方法
rMeV-MG53 保留了与亲代 MeV 相当的复制动力学,同时在 NSCLC 细胞中比未武装的对照具有显着更大的细胞毒性。从机制上讲,MG53 武装放大了 caspase-3 依赖性细胞凋亡并增强了 Gasdermin E (GSDME) 裂解,使 GSDME 介导的焦亡成为关键的肿瘤杀伤机制;药理学抑制证实 caspase 依赖性细胞凋亡是导致细胞焦亡的起始事件,同时排除坏死性凋亡和铁死亡作为 rMeV-MG53 诱导的细胞毒性的重要贡献者。
主要结果
MG53 过表达诱导了富含 TNF、NF-κB 和 IL-17 信号传导的内在促炎转录特征,而 rMeV-MG53 比未武装对照诱导了显着更强的 I 型干扰素和促炎细胞因子诱导。在免疫活性、MeV 允许的同基因 NSCLC 模型中,瘤内 rMeV-MG53 实现了卓越的肿瘤生长抑制,放大了 caspase-3/GSDME 焦亡,选择性上调 Cxcl10、Ifng 和 Il1b,并驱动强大的 CD8+ T 细胞和颗粒酶 B+ 效应器浸润。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,携带 MG53 的溶瘤药物 麻疹病毒 可以通过将焦亡肿瘤杀伤与免疫微环境重塑相结合,增强非小细胞肺癌的检查点免疫治疗,从而可能改善对当前免疫疗法耐药的患者的治疗结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在免疫功能正常、MeV 允许的同基因 NSCLC 模型中进行的,该模型可能无法完全重现人类肿瘤异质性和 免疫应答。证据强度中等,因为该研究表明了有希望的临床前结果,但需要在临床环境中进一步验证。

Engineering Oncolytic Measles Virus with MG53 Couples Pyroptotic Tumor Killing with Immune Microenvironment Remodeling to Enhance Checkpoint Immunotherapy in Non-small Cell Lung Cancer

Authors: Zhongguang Li, Cheng Chih Hsu, Fei Jiang, Matthew Bu, Umme Lubaba, Sally L. Li, Li Zhao, Pei-Hui Lin, Hunter J. Piegols, Xuefeng Liu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Lung cancer is the leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, with only approximately 30% of patients benefiting from current immune checkpoint immunotherapy. Oncolytic virotherapy offers a promising strategy to overcome this resistance; however, achieving both potent tumor cytotoxicity and robust antitumor immune activation remains a major challenge. Here, we engineered oncolytic measles virus expressing the tumor suppressor MG53 (TRIM72), designated rMeV-MG53, and evaluated its therapeutic potential against non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). rMeV-MG53 retained replication kinetics comparable to parental MeV while driving significantly greater cytotoxicity than an unarmed control in NSCLC cells. Mechanistically, MG53 arming amplified caspase-3–dependent apoptosis and potentiated Gasdermin E (GSDME) cleavage, engaging GSDME-mediated pyroptosis as a key tumor-killing mechanism; pharmacological inhibition confirmed caspase-dependent apoptosis as the initiating event leading to pyroptosis, while excluding necroptosis and ferroptosis as significant contributors to rMeV-MG53-induced cytotoxicity. MG53 overexpression induced an intrinsic pro-inflammatory transcriptional signature enriched for TNF, NF-κB, and IL-17 signaling, and rMeV-MG53 elicited significantly stronger type I interferon and pro-inflammatory cytokine induction than the unarmed control. In an immunocompetent, MeV-permissive syngeneic NSCLC model, intratumoral rMeV-MG53 achieved superior tumor growth inhibition, amplified caspase-3/GSDME pyroptosis, selectively upregulated Cxcl10 , Ifng , and Il1b , and drove robust CD8+ T-cell and Granzyme B+ effector infiltration. This immune remodeling was accompanied by compensatory PD-L1 upregulation, and combining rMeV-MG53 with anti-PD-L1 blockade achieved the strongest tumor suppression of all treatment groups. These findings establish MG53-armed oncolytic MeV as a strategy coupling GSDME-mediated pyroptosis with antitumor immune remodeling to sensitize immune-resistant NSCLC to checkpoint immunotherapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Lung cancer is the leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, with only approximately 30% of patients benefiting from current immune checkpoint immunotherapy. Oncolytic virotherapy offers a promising strategy to overcome this resistance; however, achieving both potent tumor cytotoxicity and robust antitumor immune activation remains a major challenge.

Here, we engineered oncolytic measles virus expressing the tumor suppressor MG53 (TRIM72), designated rMeV-MG53, and evaluated its therapeutic potential against non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC).

The findings suggest that MG53-armed oncolytic measles virus can enhance checkpoint immunotherapy in non-small cell lung cancer by coupling pyroptotic tumor killing with immune microenvironment remodeling, potentially improving outcomes for patients resistant to current immunotherapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in an immunocompetent, MeV-permissive syngeneic NSCLC model, which may not fully recapitulate human tumor heterogeneity and immune responses. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study demonstrates promising preclinical results but requires further validation in clinical settings.

应对 麻疹 流行病:学校可以用来促进童年的循证方法 麻疹。

Addressing the Measles Epidemic: Evidence Informed Approaches Schools Can Use to Promote Childhood Vaccination.
作者： Brian N. Fink, Brian N Fink, Fink BN.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究针对当前的 麻疹 流行病,旨在确定学校可以实施的循证方法,以促进儿童 麻疹 的发展。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了学校在 麻疹 流行期间促进儿童 麻疹 的循证策略。
核心方法
这些方法涉及回顾有关学校环境中 麻疹 暴发 和 疫苗接种 推广策略的现有文献和数据。
主要结果
该研究确定了学校可以在 麻疹 流行期间促进儿童 麻疹 的具体循证方法。
解释与创新
作者解释说,学校在 疫苗接种 推广中发挥着关键作用,并提供适合学校环境的新颖的基于证据的策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为公共卫生政策和学校干预措施提供信息,以提高 麻疹 率并控制 麻疹 暴发。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于现有文献和数据,而没有进行初步研究。

麻疹 在乌干达 18 个月以下儿童中的应用:基于案例的 麻疹 回顾性研究,2018-2024 年

Measles in Ugandan Children Under 18 Months of Age: A retrospective study of case-based surveillance, 2018–2024
作者： Gerald Businge, Ezekiel Mupere, Samuel Ofori Gyasi, Kirsty Le Doare, Michael Boele van Hensbroek, Merijn W Bijlsma, Merryn Voysey
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是乌干达的重大公共卫生威胁,定期出现 麻疹。该研究旨在调查 18 个月及以下乌干达儿童的 流行病学 或 麻疹。
设计与对象
我们分析了2018年至2024年第一季度(Q1)的全国麻疹 监测数据,探讨了人口和地理分布。该研究主要针对 18 个月及以下的儿童。目前的国家免疫计划在 9 个月时提供第一剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 疫苗 (MCV),并在 18 个月时提供第二剂。
核心方法
我们将儿童分为 6 个月以下、6 至 9 个月以下以及 9 至 18 个月。疑似 麻疹 病例被分类为临床病例、流行病学相关病例、实验室确诊病例或丢弃病例(不是 麻疹)。
主要结果
结果疑似病例中1316/2274例(57·9%)确诊为麻疹。149 例(11·3%;95% 置信区间 (CI):9·7–13·0)年龄在 6 个月以下,390 例(29·6%;27·0–32·0)6 至 9 个月以下,777 例(59·0%;56·0–62·0)9 至 18 个月。59·5% (95% CI: 55·0 - 63·0) 的 麻疹 病例是未接种疫苗的 疫苗 合格儿童。
解释与创新
解释 大多数 18 个月以下乌干达儿童中的 麻疹 病例未接种疫苗。9 至 18 个月大的婴儿占 麻疹 病例的大部分 (59·0%;777/1316)。
科研与公卫意义
The findings highlight the need for improved measles vaccination coverage among Ugandan children under 18 months. Several districts did not achieve the WHO-recommended measles surveillance threshold of at least two discarded cases per 100,000 population.
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。该研究基于国家 监测 数据并提供了详细的人口统计和地理分布,因此证据强度很高。

Measles in Ugandan Children Under 18 Months of Age: A retrospective study of case-based surveillance, 2018–2024

Authors: Gerald Businge, Ezekiel Mupere, Samuel Ofori Gyasi, Kirsty Le Doare, Michael Boele van Hensbroek, Merijn W Bijlsma, Merryn Voysey
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Measles remains a significant public health threat in Uganda, with regular outbreaks. The current national immunisation schedule offers the first measles-containing vaccine dose (MCV) at nine months, and a second dose at 18 months. We investigated the epidemiology of measles in Ugandan children aged 18 months and younger. Methods We analysed the national measles surveillance data from 2018 to the first quarter (Q1) of 2024, probing the demographic and geographic distribution. We categorised children as under six months, six to under nine months, and nine to 18 months. Suspected measles cases were classified as clinical, epidemiologically linked, laboratory-confirmed or discarded (not measles). Results 1316/2274 (57·9%) of the suspected cases were diagnosed with measles. 149 cases (11·3%; 95% Confidence Interval (CI): 9·7–13·0) were aged under six months, 390 (29·6%; 27·0–32·0) six to under nine months, and 777 (59·0%; 56·0–62·0) nine to 18 months. 717/1208 (59·4%; 57·0–62·0) of vaccine-eligible children, with a captured vaccination history, had received at least one dose of MCV. 59·5% (95%CI: 55·0 - 63·0) of measles cases were unvaccinated vaccine-eligible children. Several districts did not achieve the WHO-recommended measles surveillance threshold of at least two discarded cases per 100,000 population. Interpretation Most measles cases among Ugandan children ≤ 18 months are unvaccinated. Infants nine to 18 months old comprise most of the measles cases (59·0%; 777/1316). To achieve better control of measles transmission, review of the current vaccination policies with strengthened vaccination and surveillance systems are recommended.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a significant public health threat in Uganda, with regular outbreaks. The study aimed to investigate the epidemiology of measles in Ugandan children aged 18 months and younger.
Design and population
We analysed the national measles surveillance data from 2018 to the first quarter (Q1) of 2024, probing the demographic and geographic distribution. The study focused on children aged 18 months and younger. The current national immunisation schedule offers the first measles-containing vaccine dose (MCV) at nine months, and a second dose at 18 months.
Core methods
We categorised children as under six months, six to under nine months, and nine to 18 months. Suspected measles cases were classified as clinical, epidemiologically linked, laboratory-confirmed or discarded (not measles).
Main results
Results 1316/2274 (57·9%) of the suspected cases were diagnosed with measles. 149 cases (11·3%; 95% Confidence Interval (CI): 9·7–13·0) were aged under six months, 390 (29·6%; 27·0–32·0) six to under nine months, and 777 (59·0%; 56·0–62·0) nine to 18 months. 59·5% (95%CI: 55·0 - 63·0) of measles cases were unvaccinated vaccine-eligible children.
Interpretation and novelty
Interpretation Most measles cases among Ugandan children ≤ 18 months are unvaccinated. Infants nine to 18 months old comprise most of the measles cases (59·0%; 777/1316).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved measles vaccination coverage among Ugandan children under 18 months. Several districts did not achieve the WHO-recommended measles surveillance threshold of at least two discarded cases per 100,000 population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study is based on national surveillance data and provides detailed demographic and geographic distribution.

儿科 麻疹 和内脏利什曼病合并感染:诊断挑战和病理生理学见解

Pediatric Measles and Visceral Leishmaniasis Co-infection: Diagnostic Challenges and Insights Into Pathophysiology
作者： Kalliopi Straka, Eleni Christakou
摘要中文翻译尽管存在有效的 疫苗,但 麻疹 仍然是全球儿童发病的主要原因。除了其急性临床表现外,麻疹 还会引起严重但短暂的免疫功能障碍,使受影响的儿童容易发生继发性和机会性感染。内脏利什曼病 (VL) 是一种由利什曼原虫引起的全身性原虫感染,如果不及时治疗,其特征是长期发热、肝脾肿大、全血细胞减少和高 死亡率。由于临床和实验室特征重叠,严重 麻疹 和 VL 的共存带来了重大的诊断挑战。我们报告了一名先前健康的三岁男孩入住儿科重症监护室的病例,该男孩患有严重的 PCR 确诊的 麻疹 并发急性呼吸衰竭和血流动力学不稳定,需要有创机械通气和血管活性支持。尽管进行了初步强化治疗,但持续发热、进行性肝脾肿大和血细胞减少促使进一步研究,结果显示 rK39 抗原检测呈阳性,抗利什曼原虫抗体滴度升高,建立了 VL 的 诊断。该患者经过脂质体两性霉素 B、补充维生素 A、静脉注射免疫球蛋白和支持性重症监护的成功治疗,最终临床完全康复,没有留下后遗症。据我们所知,之前没有 麻疹-VL 联合感染的儿科病例记录。该病例强调了严重 麻疹 儿童持续性全身炎症和血细胞减少的诊断复杂性,并说明了当临床病程不典型或延长时考虑地方性机会性感染的重要性。良好的结果强调了早期识别、及时针对病原体的治疗和多学科管理的价值,同时强化了 麻疹 疫苗接种 在预防严重疾病及其感染并发症方面的关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在有效的 麻疹,但 麻疹 仍然是全球儿童发病的主要原因,其免疫功能障碍使受影响的儿童容易发生继发性和机会性感染,当发生内脏利什曼病等合并感染时,会给诊断带来挑战。
设计与对象
我们报告了一名先前健康的三岁男孩入住儿科重症监护室的病例,该男孩患有严重的 PCR 确诊的 麻疹 并发急性呼吸衰竭和血流动力学不稳定,需要有创机械通气和血管活性支持。
核心方法
尽管进行了初步强化治疗,但持续发热、进行性肝脾肿大和血细胞减少促使进一步研究,结果显示 rK39 抗原检测呈阳性,抗利什曼原虫抗体滴度升高,建立了 VL 的 诊断。
主要结果
该患者经过脂质体两性霉素 B、补充维生素 A、静脉注射免疫球蛋白和支持性重症监护的成功治疗,最终临床完全康复,没有留下后遗症。
解释与创新
据我们所知,之前没有记录到 麻疹-VL 合并感染的儿科病例,这突显了严重 麻疹 儿童持续性全身炎症和血细胞减少的诊断复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例说明了当临床病程不典型或延长时考虑地方性机会性感染的重要性,同时强调早期识别、及时针对病原体的治疗和多学科管理的价值。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为这是单个 病例报告,这限制了普遍性,但详细的临床文件和确诊的诊断为这种罕见的合并感染提供了强有力的见解。

Pediatric Measles and Visceral Leishmaniasis Co-infection: Diagnostic Challenges and Insights Into Pathophysiology

Authors: Kalliopi Straka, Eleni Christakou
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a major cause of childhood morbidity worldwide despite the availability of an effective vaccine. Beyond its acute clinical manifestations, measles induces profound but transient immune dysfunction, predisposing affected children to secondary and opportunistic infections. Visceral leishmaniasis (VL) is a systemic protozoal infection caused by Leishmania species and is characterized by prolonged fever, hepatosplenomegaly, pancytopenia, and high mortality if left untreated. The coexistence of severe measles and VL poses a significant diagnostic challenge because of their overlapping clinical and laboratory features. We report the case of a previously healthy three-year-old boy admitted to the pediatric intensive care unit with severe PCR-confirmed measles complicated by acute respiratory failure and hemodynamic instability requiring invasive mechanical ventilation and vasoactive support. Persistent fever, progressive hepatosplenomegaly, and cytopenias despite initial intensive management prompted further investigation, which demonstrated positive rK39 antigen testing and elevated anti-Leishmania antibody titers, establishing the diagnosis of VL. The patient was successfully treated with liposomal amphotericin B, vitamin A supplementation, IV immunoglobulin, and supportive intensive care, resulting in complete clinical recovery without sequelae. To the best of our knowledge, no pediatric case of measles-VL co-infection has been documented previously. This case highlights the diagnostic complexity of persistent systemic inflammation and cytopenias in children with severe measles and illustrates the importance of considering endemic opportunistic infections when the clinical course is atypical or prolonged. The favorable outcome emphasizes the value of early recognition, timely pathogen-directed therapy, and multidisciplinary management, while reinforcing the critical role of measles vaccination in preventing severe disease and its infectious complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a major cause of childhood morbidity worldwide despite the availability of an effective vaccine, and its immune dysfunction predisposes affected children to secondary and opportunistic infections, creating diagnostic challenges when co-infections like visceral leishmaniasis occur.
Design and population
We report the case of a previously healthy three-year-old boy admitted to the pediatric intensive care unit with severe PCR-confirmed measles complicated by acute respiratory failure and hemodynamic instability requiring invasive mechanical ventilation and vasoactive support.
Core methods
Persistent fever, progressive hepatosplenomegaly, and cytopenias despite initial intensive management prompted further investigation, which demonstrated positive rK39 antigen testing and elevated anti-Leishmania antibody titers, establishing the diagnosis of VL.
Main results
The patient was successfully treated with liposomal amphotericin B, vitamin A supplementation, IV immunoglobulin, and supportive intensive care, resulting in complete clinical recovery without sequelae.
Interpretation and novelty
To the best of our knowledge, no pediatric case of measles-VL co-infection has been documented previously, highlighting the diagnostic complexity of persistent systemic inflammation and cytopenias in children with severe measles.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case illustrates the importance of considering endemic opportunistic infections when the clinical course is atypical or prolonged, while emphasizing the value of early recognition, timely pathogen-directed therapy, and multidisciplinary management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as this is a single case report, which limits generalizability, but the detailed clinical documentation and confirmed diagnoses provide robust insights into this rare co-infection.

巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的调查(2022 年至 2023 年):探索合并症、并发症和分子特征

Investigation of measles outbreak in Pakistan (2022 to 2023): Exploring comorbidities, complications, and molecular characterization
作者： Asia Nawaz, Sidra Rahman, Nighat Haider, Adnan Zeb, Muhammad Ali
摘要中文翻译背景 麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖和医疗基础设施不足的地区,例如巴基斯坦。本研究的目的是调查 2022 年至 2023 年巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的流行病学、分子和系统发育特征,并强调有助于更好地了解该疾病的改进领域,并为有效的 麻疹 管理和 疫苗接种 优化提供策略。方法 收集2022年12月至2023年12月巴基斯坦伊斯兰堡PIMS收治的患者(n = 183)的咽拭子。使用SPSS-21软件对数据进行统计分析。利用不同的生物信息学软件进行系统发育、氨基酸取代及其功能分析以及部分核蛋白的结构分析。结果 大多数 麻疹 患者是具有较高 患病率 合并症和并发症的婴儿,并且大量患者的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围不足。系统发育分析揭示了与俄罗斯和美国流行的 B3 菌株的相关性,强调了全球传播。氨基酸取代和结构分析强调了当前分离株与参考菌株相比的微小结构变化。结论 研究结果强调了持续 麻疹 死亡率 和了解 麻疹 流行病学 和病毒变异对于有效 暴发 管理和 疫苗接种 优化的重要性,最终旨在减轻巴基斯坦和全球类似环境中与 麻疹 死亡率 相关的发病率和 死亡率。这项研究例证了跨学科合作在应对复杂传染病挑战方面的重要性,强调了通过 麻疹 对抗 麻疹 进行持续监测和准备的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖和医疗基础设施不足的地区,例如巴基斯坦。
设计与对象
本研究的目的是调查 2022 年至 2023 年巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的流行病学、分子和系统发育特征,并强调有助于更好地了解该疾病的改进领域,并为有效的 麻疹 管理和 疫苗接种 优化提供策略。
核心方法
咽拭子采集自 2022 年 12 月至 2023 年 12 月在巴基斯坦伊斯兰堡 PIMS 收治的患者 (n = 183)。使用 SPSS-21 软件对数据进行统计分析。
主要结果
大多数 麻疹 患者是 患病率 合并症和并发症较高的婴儿,并且大量患者的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围不足。系统发育分析揭示了与俄罗斯和美国流行的 B3 菌株的相关性,强调了全球传播。氨基酸取代和结构分析强调了当前分离株与参考菌株相比的微小结构变化。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了持续 监测 以及理解 麻疹、流行病学 和病毒变异对于有效的 暴发 管理和 疫苗接种 优化的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进 疫苗接种 策略和 监测 系统,以减轻巴基斯坦和全球类似环境中与 麻疹 死亡率 相关的发病率和 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Investigation of measles outbreak in Pakistan (2022 to 2023): Exploring comorbidities, complications, and molecular characterization

Authors: Asia Nawaz, Sidra Rahman, Nighat Haider, Adnan Zeb, Muhammad Ali
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Measles remains a major public health concern worldwide, particularly in regions with insufficient vaccination coverage and healthcare infrastructure, like Pakistan. The purpose of this study is to investigate the epidemiological, molecular, and phylogenetic characteristics of the measles outbreak in Pakistan during 2022-2023 and highlight the areas of improvement that can contribute to the better understanding of the disease and inform strategies for effective outbreak management and vaccination optimization. METHODS Throat swabs were collected from patients (n = 183) admitted at PIMS, Islamabad, Pakistan, from December 2022- December 2023. Statistical analysis of data was conducted on SPSS-21 software. The phylogenetics, amino acid substitutions, their functional analysis, and structural analysis of partial nucleoprotein were performed by using different bioinformatics software. RESULTS Most of the measles patients were infants with a high prevalence of comorbidity and complications, and significant number of patients had inadequate vaccination coverage. The phylogenetic analysis revealed relatedness to B3 strains circulating in Russia and the USA, emphasizing the global spread. The amino acid substitutions and structure analysis highlighted minor structural variations in current isolates as compared to the reference strain. CONCLUSION The findings underscore the importance of continuous surveillance and understanding of measles epidemiology and viral variations for effective outbreak management and vaccination optimization, ultimately aiming to mitigate the morbidity and mortality associated with measles outbreaks in Pakistan and similar settings globally. This study exemplifies the significance of interdisciplinary collaboration in confronting complex infectious disease challenges, emphasizing the imperative of continuous monitoring and preparedness in combating measles through vaccination.

Question and background

Design and population

Core methods

Most of the measles patients were infants with a high prevalence of comorbidity and complications, and significant number of patients had inadequate vaccination coverage. The phylogenetic analysis revealed relatedness to B3 strains circulating in Russia and the USA, emphasizing the global spread. The amino acid substitutions and structure analysis highlighted minor structural variations in current isolates as compared to the reference strain.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved vaccination strategies and surveillance systems to mitigate the morbidity and mortality associated with measles outbreaks in Pakistan and similar settings globally.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

具有不同易感性和传染性的离散状态结构流行病模型及其在美国 麻疹 暴发 中的应用

Discrete state-structured epidemic model with varying susceptibility and infectivity and its application to the measles outbreak in the United States
作者： Wenxuan Li, Chiyu Zhang, Xu Chen, Guyue Liu, Suli Liu
摘要中文翻译患有传染病 宿主 的个体经常在不同的易感性和传染性状态之间转变。因此,我们推导出一类流行病模型,其中易感者和感染者分别具有一组离散的易感性和感染性状态。我们的模型基于双剂量 疫苗接种,并考虑了状态之间所有可能的状态转换。对所提出的模型进行了全面的数学分析,包括评估控制繁殖数和解决方案的全局动态行为。使用改进的仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗方法,我们还用美国 麻疹 案例数据拟合模型。结果表明,尽管疫苗接种覆盖率和有效率较高,但零星或小规模的麻疹 暴发仍然存在。这表明仅依赖疫苗接种政策不足以完全中断其传播链。因此,在现有 麻疹 工作的基础上,提高 麻疹 案件的 麻疹 率是控制 麻疹、传播 和 暴发 的另一项关键措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对流行病模型的需求,该模型可以解释接种疫苗的个体中不同的易感性和传染性状态,该模型应用于美国的 麻疹 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个数学模型,并将其与来自美国的 麻疹 案例数据进行拟合。
核心方法
推导出离散状态结构流行病模型,其中包含双剂量 疫苗接种 和所有可能的状态转换。该模型经过数学分析,包括控制再生数和全局动态,并使用改进的仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗方法拟合到 麻疹 案例数据。
主要结果
结果表明,尽管疫苗接种覆盖率和有效率较高,但零星或小规模的麻疹 暴发仍然存在。
解释与创新
作者解释说,仅依赖 疫苗接种 政策不足以完全中断 麻疹 传播,强调需要采取额外措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在现有疫苗接种工作的基础上,提高麻疹案件的诊断率是控制传播和暴发的重要补充措施。
局限与证据强度
证据仅基于摘要,模型验证和数据源的细节有限,导致可信度较低。

Discrete state-structured epidemic model with varying susceptibility and infectivity and its application to the measles outbreak in the United States

Authors: Wenxuan Li, Chiyu Zhang, Xu Chen, Guyue Liu, Suli Liu
Original abstract (English)
Host individuals with vaccinations for infectious diseases often transition among different states of susceptibility and infectivity. Thus, we derive a class of epidemic models in which susceptible and infected individuals have a discrete set of susceptibility and infectivity states, respectively. Our model is based on double-dose vaccination and accounts for all possible state transitions between states. A comprehensive mathematical analysis of the proposed model is conducted, including assessing the control reproduction number and the global dynamical behaviors of the solutions. Using an improved affine-invariant ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo method, we also fit the model with measles case data in the United States. The results indicate that despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy rates, sporadic or small-scale measles outbreaks still occur. This suggests that reliance solely on vaccination policies is insufficient to fully interrupt its transmission chain. Consequently, enhancing the diagnosis rate of measles cases is another crucial measure for controlling measles transmission and outbreaks, building upon existing vaccination efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for epidemic models that account for varying susceptibility and infectivity states among vaccinated individuals, applied to measles outbreaks in the United States.
Design and population
The study develops a mathematical model and fits it to measles case data from the United States.
Core methods
A discrete state-structured epidemic model is derived, incorporating double-dose vaccination and all possible state transitions. The model is analyzed mathematically, including the control reproduction number and global dynamics, and fitted to measles case data using an improved affine-invariant ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo method.
Main results
The results indicate that despite high vaccination coverage and efficacy rates, sporadic or small-scale measles outbreaks still occur.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that reliance solely on vaccination policies is insufficient to fully interrupt measles transmission, highlighting the need for additional measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that enhancing the diagnosis rate of measles cases is a crucial supplementary measure for controlling transmission and outbreaks, building upon existing vaccination efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract only, with limited detail on model validation and data sources, resulting in low confidence.

多学科儿科医院的院内 麻疹 传播

Nosocomial measles transmission in a multidisciplinary pediatric hospital
作者： Yu. A. Gaist, А. В. Любимова, L. V. Belova, E. N. Kolosovskaya, A. V. Liubimova
摘要中文翻译找出儿科综合医院院内麻疹 传播的原因。材料和方法。他的研究对象包括 180 名患者、158 名住院儿童的护理人员以及一家儿科医院的 73 名医护人员。麻疹 案例识别是使用标准案例定义进行的。实验室确认采用酶联免疫吸附法检测血清中麻疹特异性IgM和IgG抗体,采用聚合酶链反应鉴别呼吸道病毒(鼻咽拭子)麻疹。一名9个月大的女性患者出现典型的麻疹临床表现;尽管如此,诊断 是在住院 5 小时后建立的。使用“Ariadna”医疗信息系统识别联系人(患者、家长/监护人),根据部门人员配置记录识别医护人员。2024年3月25日至2024年3月30日期间,在与感染患者共用通风系统的同一病房或相邻房间内的其他住院患者中发现了接触者。由于诊断延迟,确定了411名接触者,4名患者因麻疹的诊断而再次住院。病毒疹需要提高诊断警惕性,特别是在 发病率 等高传染性感染的流行期间。有必要改进多学科医院中出现发疹性疾病的患者路由算法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查多学科儿科医院院内 麻疹 传播 背后的原因,强调需要了解 麻疹 在医疗机构中如何传播。
设计与对象
该研究包括 180 名患者、158 名住院儿童的护理人员以及一家儿科医院的 73 名医护人员。2024年3月25日至2024年3月30日期间,在与感染患者共用通风系统的同一病房或相邻房间内的其他住院患者中发现了接触者。
核心方法
麻疹 案例识别是使用标准案例定义进行的。实验室确认采用酶联免疫吸附法检测血清中麻疹特异性IgM和IgG抗体,采用聚合酶链反应鉴别呼吸道病毒(鼻咽拭子)麻疹。
主要结果
一名9个月大的女性患者出现典型的麻疹临床表现;尽管如此,诊断 是在住院 5 小时后建立的。由于诊断延迟,确定了411名接触者,4名患者因麻疹的诊断而再次住院。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了及时 诊断 在预防院内 麻疹 传播 方面的重要性,特别是在患者路由算法可能需要改进的多学科医院中。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,在 发病率 等高传染性感染(例如 麻疹)流行期间,需要提高诊断警惕性,并表明改进发疹性疾病患者路由算法的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖延迟的 诊断 来识别接触者而受到限制,这可能会影响 传播 追踪的准确性。证据是中等的,因为它基于仅摘要的审查。

Nosocomial measles transmission in a multidisciplinary pediatric hospital

Authors: Yu. A. Gaist, А. В. Любимова, L. V. Belova, E. N. Kolosovskaya, A. V. Liubimova
Original abstract (English)
To find out reasons of nosocomial measles transmission in the multidisciplinary pediatric hospital. Materials and methods. He study included 180 patients, 158 caregivers of hospitalized children, and 73 healthcare workers of a pediatric hospital. Measles case identification was performed using a standard case definition. Laboratory confirmation was carried out using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay to detect measles-specific IgM and IgG antibodies in blood serum, and polymerase chain reaction were used for differential diagnosis of respiratory viruses (nasopharyngeal swab). A 9-month-old female patient presented with a typical clinical picture of measles; nevertheless, the diagnosis was established 5 hours after hospitalisation. Contact persons (patients, parents/guardians) were identified using the «Ariadna» medical information system, healthcare workers were identified according on departmental staffing records. Contacts were identified among other hospitalized persons from March 25, 2024, to March 30, 2024, in the same wards or adjacent rooms sharing a common ventilation system with the infected patient. Due to delayed diagnosis, 411 contacts were identified, and 4 patients were re-hospitalised with a diagnosis of measles. Viral exanthems require heightened diagnostic vigilance, particularly during epidemic rises in incidence of highly contagious infections such as measles. Improvement of patient routing algorithms with exanthematous diseases in multidisciplinary hospitals is warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the reasons behind nosocomial measles transmission in a multidisciplinary pediatric hospital, highlighting the need for understanding how measles spreads within healthcare settings.
Design and population
The study included 180 patients, 158 caregivers of hospitalized children, and 73 healthcare workers from a pediatric hospital. Contacts were identified among other hospitalized persons from March 25, 2024, to March 30, 2024, in the same wards or adjacent rooms sharing a common ventilation system with the infected patient.
Core methods
Measles case identification was performed using a standard case definition. Laboratory confirmation was carried out using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay to detect measles-specific IgM and IgG antibodies in blood serum, and polymerase chain reaction were used for differential diagnosis of respiratory viruses (nasopharyngeal swab).
Main results
A 9-month-old female patient presented with a typical clinical picture of measles; nevertheless, the diagnosis was established 5 hours after hospitalisation. Due to delayed diagnosis, 411 contacts were identified, and 4 patients were re-hospitalised with a diagnosis of measles.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings underscore the importance of timely diagnosis in preventing nosocomial measles transmission, particularly in multidisciplinary hospitals where patient routing algorithms may need improvement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for heightened diagnostic vigilance during epidemic rises in incidence of highly contagious infections such as measles, and suggest the importance of improving patient routing algorithms for exanthematous diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on delayed diagnosis for identifying contacts, which may affect the accuracy of transmission tracing. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only review.

麻疹 媒介 附加转录单元非翻译区的微小差异通过编码的 MERS-CoV Spike 抗原的不同免疫原性反映出来

Minor differences in the untranslated regions of measles vector additional transcription units are reflected by differential immunogenicity of encoded MERS-CoV Spike antigen
作者： Vishaka Tiwarekar, Aileen Ebenig, Yvonne Predota, Sabrina Schrauf, Cindy Hörner, Mona V. Lange, Mohamed R. Gadalla, Arne Auste, Csaba Miskey, Samada Muraleedharan
摘要中文翻译摘要 重组 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 是 疫苗 平台技术之一,该技术经过测试可快速生成针对其他病原体(例如新出现的病毒性疾病)的有效 疫苗。不同的减毒活毒株 疫苗 已成为主干毒株,几乎没有差异。在这里,我们分析了重组 Moraten (MV vac2 ) 或 Schwarz (MV Schw ) 构建体的微小差异如何影响各自衍生的中东呼吸综合征 (MERS) 疫苗 候选者的性能。MV vac2 或 MV Schw 病毒均在血凝素基因和聚合酶基因 L 盒之间的附加转录单元 (ATU) 中编码 MERS 冠状病毒 (MERS-CoV) 的刺突糖蛋白 (S)。两者都强烈表达了额外的基因。然而,在被 MV Schw -MERS-S(H) 病毒感染的细胞中观察到大约两倍至三倍的 S 蛋白含量,这与这些细胞中发现的 S mRNA 的相同相对比例相关。将 MV vac2 中的短基因组 3' UTR ATU 序列与 MV Schw 中的序列进行交换,以大约相同的因子增强了基因表达,并证明了因果关系。接种任一候选疫苗的 MeV 敏感 IFNAR -/− -CD46Ge 小鼠显示,MV Schw -MERS-S(H) 的较高相对 S 抗原表达倾向于诱导较高的特异性抗体滴度,但 S 抗原特异性 T 细胞数量较低。在随后的挑战实验中,两种候选药物均有效。因此,重组 MeV 的 ATU 遗传组成的微小差异可能会调节额外编码蛋白质的基因表达模式,这些蛋白质通过各自免疫原性的变化反映出来,但这对于所选疾病模型的保护并不是决定性的。重要性 如果出现新出现或重新出现的感染,需要 疫苗 平台技术来快速开发有效的 疫苗 以帮助流行病中的公共医疗保健。除了 mRNA 疫苗 之外,病毒平台技术,即腺病毒衍生的 疫苗 Vaxzevria 和 JCOVDEN,已被证明在 COVID-19 大流行期间具有巨大价值。对于未来的大流行,了解 媒介 设计中调节免疫原性的因素至关重要。这些知识允许定制 疫苗 媒介 以适应特定的目标产品概况,例如,构建 媒介 来触发增强的 T 细胞或针对感兴趣抗原的抗体反应。因此,我们使用减毒活 麻疹 疫苗 主链作为有希望的例子的研究对于证明 疫苗 主链中非常微小的差异可以改变 媒介 抗原系统的抗原表达谱并影响针对添加的靶抗原的 T 细胞或抗体反应的相对诱导至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 麻疹病毒 媒介 附加转录单元的非翻译区域的微小差异如何影响编码的 MERS-CoV Spike 抗原的免疫原性,解决针对未来大流行优化 疫苗 媒介 设计的需要。
设计与对象
该研究比较了两种重组 麻疹病毒 菌株 MV vac2 和 MV Schw,它们在额外的转录单元中编码 MERS-CoV Spike 糖蛋白,使用 MeV 敏感的 IFNAR−/−-CD46Ge 小鼠进行 疫苗接种 和攻击实验。
核心方法
测试 MV vac2 和 MV Schw 病毒的 Spike 蛋白表达,并将 MV vac2 的 3' UTR ATU 序列与 MV Schw 的序列交换以评估因果关系。在接种疫苗的小鼠中测量抗体滴度和 T 细胞反应,然后进行攻击实验。
主要结果
与 MV vac2 相比,MV Schw-MERS-S(H) 显示出大约两倍至三倍的 Spike 蛋白表达和 mRNA 水平,3'UTR 序列交换也类似地增强了表达。疫苗接种 与 MV Schw-MERS-S(H) 倾向于诱导较高的抗体滴度,但较低的 T 细胞反应,尽管两种候选物在攻击实验中都提供了有效的保护。
解释与创新
作者解释说,麻疹病毒 主链中的微小遗传差异可以调节抗原表达和免疫原性,研究表明这种差异会影响 T 细胞与抗体反应的平衡,而不影响模型中的保护功效。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,微妙的基因修饰可以定制疫苗,从而为病毒媒介 疫苗的设计提供信息,从而能够开发针对特定免疫结果优化的疫苗,例如增强针对目标抗原的抗体或T细胞反应。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在单一动物模型中进行的,并且重点关注一组有限的免疫原性结果,尽管遗传差异和免疫原性之间的直接因果关系提供了强有力的机制见解。

Minor differences in the untranslated regions of measles vector additional transcription units are reflected by differential immunogenicity of encoded MERS-CoV Spike antigen

Authors: Vishaka Tiwarekar, Aileen Ebenig, Yvonne Predota, Sabrina Schrauf, Cindy Hörner, Mona V. Lange, Mohamed R. Gadalla, Arne Auste, Csaba Miskey, Samada Muraleedharan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The recombinant measles virus (MeV) is among those vaccine platform technologies, which are tested for the fast generation of effective vaccines against other pathogens, such as emerging virus diseases. Different live-attenuated vaccine strains have become available as backbones, with few differences. Here, we analyze how minor differences of recombinant Moraten (MV vac2 ) or Schwarz (MV Schw ) constructs have an impact on the performance of respective derived Middle East respiratory syndrome (MERS) vaccine candidates. MV vac2 or MV Schw viruses, both encoding the Spike glycoprotein (S) of the MERS-coronavirus (MERS-CoV) in an additional transcription unit (ATU) between the hemagglutinin gene and the polymerase gene L cassette, were tested. Both robustly expressed the additional gene. However, approximately twofold to threefold higher amounts of S proteins were observed in cells infected by the MV Schw -MERS-S(H) virus, correlating with the same relative ratio of S mRNA found in these cells. Exchange of the short genomic 3′ UTR ATU sequence in MV vac2 against the sequence in MV Schw enhanced gene expression by about the same factor and demonstrates causality. MeV-susceptible IFNAR −/− -CD46Ge mice vaccinated with either candidate reveal that higher relative S antigen expression by MV Schw -MERS-S(H) tended toward induction of higher specific antibody titers, but lower numbers of S antigen-specific T cells. In subsequent challenge experiments, both candidates were effective. Thus, minor differences in the genetic composition of recombinant MeV’s ATU may modulate the gene expression pattern of additionally encoded proteins that are reflected by changes in the respective immunogenicities, but which were not decisive for protection in the chosen disease model. IMPORTANCE In case of emerging or re-emerging infections, vaccine platform technologies are needed to rapidly develop effective vaccines to aid public healthcare in pandemics. Besides mRNA vaccines, also viral platform technologies, that is, the adenovirus-derived vaccines Vaxzevria and JCOVDEN, have proven to be of immense value during the COVID-19 pandemic. For future pandemics, it is crucial to understand the factors in vector design that modulate immunogenicity. This knowledge allows the tailoring of vaccine vectors to fit specific target product profiles, for example, to build vectors which trigger an accentuated T cell or, alternatively, antibody response against an antigen of interest. Our study using the live-attenuated measles vaccine backbone as a promising example is therefore crucial in demonstrating that very minor differences in the vaccine backbone can alter the antigen expression profile of the vector-antigen system and impact the relative induction of T-cell or antibody responses against the added target antigen
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how minor differences in the untranslated regions of measles virus vector additional transcription units impact the immunogenicity of encoded MERS-CoV Spike antigen, addressing the need to optimize vaccine vector design for future pandemics.
Design and population
The study compared two recombinant measles virus strains, MV vac2 and MV Schw, encoding the MERS-CoV Spike glycoprotein in an additional transcription unit, using MeV-susceptible IFNAR−/−-CD46Ge mice for vaccination and challenge experiments.
Core methods
MV vac2 and MV Schw viruses were tested for Spike protein expression, and the 3′ UTR ATU sequence of MV vac2 was exchanged with that of MV Schw to assess causality. Antibody titers and T-cell responses were measured in vaccinated mice, followed by challenge experiments.
Main results
MV Schw-MERS-S(H) showed approximately twofold to threefold higher Spike protein expression and mRNA levels compared to MV vac2, with the 3′ UTR sequence exchange enhancing expression similarly. Vaccination with MV Schw-MERS-S(H) tended to induce higher antibody titers but lower T-cell responses, though both candidates provided effective protection in challenge experiments.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that minor genetic differences in the measles virus backbone can modulate antigen expression and immunogenicity, with the study demonstrating that such differences impact the balance of T-cell versus antibody responses without affecting protective efficacy in the model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the design of viral vector vaccines by showing that subtle genetic modifications can tailor immune responses, enabling the development of vaccines optimized for specific immune outcomes, such as enhanced antibody or T-cell responses against target antigens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in a single animal model and focused on a limited set of immunogenicity outcomes, though the direct causal link between genetic differences and immunogenicity provides robust mechanistic insight.
